Text:
Regular Full-Time Employee, **Description** = An employee working the standard full-time schedule under a regular employment agreement.. Regular Part-Time Employee, **Description** = An employee working fewer hours than the standard full-time schedule under a part-time employment agreement.. Probationary Employee, **Description** = A newly hired employee undergoing the company's initial assessment period before confirmation.. Intern, **Description** = A student or recent graduate engaged for a defined learning and work experience period.. Trainee, **Description** = An individual engaged in a structured training program before being assigned to a regular role.. Fixed-Term Employee, **Description** = An employee hired for a specified period under a fixed-term employment agreement.. Contract Employee, **Description** = An individual engaged through a third-party staffing or contracting organization.
